Validated: checkpoint-1/2 saved in dry-run.\\n\\n(B) Reward weighting: all 3 rewards (format/f1/ced) were equally summed by TRL → added WeightedRewardFn wrapper + --format_reward_weight/--f1_reward_weight/--ced_reward_weight CLI args. Also added --max_steps, --kl_coef (beta). Dry-run clip_ratio=0 expected (zero reward variance → zero advantage → no update needed).\\n\\n(D) Added --save_total_limit CLI arg (previously hardcoded to 2, now configurable).\\n\\nDry-run validated: 2 steps completed, checkpoints saved, no OOM with num_iterations=2 on 4xRTX5090. completions/clipped_ratio=1.0 (all hit max_completion_length=1024) — expected for cold-start without
